Native-thinking causal site review

Frozen parser/cohort/token-site decisions. This artifact does not run or report causal interventions.

AUDIT PASS
600trajectories
3215events
446primary trajectories
154scope/exclusion rows

Decision hierarchy

surface grammar selects token roles; trajectory cohort selects the estimand. Cohort membership never moves a site.

semantic rolefrozen rule
semantic_span_normalizationstrip parser-edge Unicode whitespace from semantic item, city-unit, and rank-evidence surface; additionally strip edge punctuation from rank evidence when an alphanumeric core remains; retain all original/surface/core boundaries; do not trim city or Total:
retrieval_transitiontransition k -> k+1; every anchor scores exactly the same next-city token span, while intervening text is teacher-forced but not scored
p0_item_endlast token of committed item k; earliest event-specific anchor for city k+1
unit_pre_d1token immediately before the target city-bearing semantic unit
pre_marker_d1token immediately before an explicit marker only when that marker precedes the target city
post_markerlast marker-core token only when that marker precedes the target city
post_open_delimiterlast token of a separable opening bracket or invariant bullet; fused delimiter/content tokens are explicit N/A
record_clause_pre_d1token immediately before the literal clause In the 2024 city score audit when present in the target event
city_pre_d1output token immediately before the first token overlapping the target city span
block_pre_d1token before the first compiled event; exploratory non-event-specific anchor, excluded from primary pooling
anchor_equivalencesemantic roles resolving to one model token are one intervention with all aliases retained; they are never double counted
city_target_spansmallest output-token interval overlapping the full registered city surface
rank_evidence_core_spantokens overlapping semantic ordinal/count content after whitespace and removable edge-punctuation normalization; causal marker state uses this span
rank_evidence_surface_spantokens overlapping the whitespace-trimmed parser evidence surface; retained for visible-surface removal controls and audit
rank_visible_format_shell_tokenstokens in rank_evidence_surface_span but outside rank_evidence_core_span; paired visible syntax/delimiter control, allowed to be empty
pre_marker_stateoutput token immediately before the first token overlapping rank_evidence_core_span
post_marker_statelast output token overlapping rank_evidence_core_span
post_update_commit_statelast output token overlapping the selected event semantic span
continue_to_next_cityall tokens after current commit through next-city end, with next-city-only tokens saved separately
stop_to_answer_query_v3all tokens after final observed commit through the independent answer_query_v3 boundary
semantic_target_onlyfor retrieval-anchor localization, only the identical next-city token span enters the primary score; interstitial distance is retained as a diagnostic
prompt_source_recordthe frozen full prompt record span registered for the event city
cohortallowed estimand
primary_rank_resolved_full_chainrank_supported_episode plus one_to_one; eligible for retrieval, progress transition, terminal stop, and later serial mediation
secondary_structural_marker_neutralone_to_one structural fallback; marker-neutral/structural sensitivity, not pooled into primary rank-resolved effects
secondary_structural_recapstructural extension of an earlier ranked episode; recap/consolidation sensitivity only
secondary_local_partial_uniquelocal observed retrieval and continue transitions only; no correct terminal-count estimand
occurrence_retrieval_only_duplicatessource retrieval at each emitted occurrence only; no unique-count/progress claim
secondary_evidence_sequence_exploratoryexact unique coverage recovered from score-supported first mentions; exploratory retrieval, continue, and terminal stop; occurrence index is not rank evidence
secondary_evidence_sequence_partial_exploratorypartial score-supported first-mention sequence; exploratory local retrieval and continue only; no terminal-count or rank-marker claim
